./bruno-constant-deep-dive.html-116-      <p class="text-slate-700 leading-relaxed mb-4">In the Bruno Framework, entropy is primary. Temperature is treated as a proxy for accessible degrees of freedom. The Bruno Constant, κ, marks the threshold at which a system undergoes a dimensional transition—from 3D volume to a 2D surface-projected state—stabilizing structure and enabling emergent properties (mass, spin, charge, and apparent gravity) to manifest as consequences of entropic geometry.</p>
